                                                         Oatmeal Thumbprints
Ingredients                       Directions
1/2 cup butter, softened         Preheat oven to 400 degrees F (205 degrees C).
1/2 cup shortening
1 cup packed brown sugar         Cream butter and shortening with sugars. Beat in eggs. In a
3/4 cup white sugar              separate bowl, sift together flour, baking soda, salt, nuts and
2 eggs                           cinnamon. Add to butter mixture alternately with water. Stir in oats
2 1/2 cups all-purpose flour     and almond extract.
1 teaspoon baking soda
                                 Drop by teaspoons on ungreased cookie sheets. Make a small
1 teaspoon salt
                                 indentation in each cookie. Fill with preserves.
1/2 teaspoon ground cinnamon
1/2 cup water                    Bake for 10-12 minutes.
2 1/2 cups quick cooking oats
1/2 cup finely chopped walnuts
1 teaspoon almond extract
1/4 cup raspberry jam

                                                Cranberry Oatmeal Drops
Ingredients                      Directions
1 cup all-purpose flour         Preheat oven to 375 degrees F (190 degrees C). Grease cookie
1 teaspoon baking powder        sheets.
1/2 teaspoon ground cinnamon
1/4 teaspoon ground nutmeg      Sift together the flour, baking powder, cinnamon and nutmeg, set
1/2 cup butter, softened        aside. In a medium bowl, cream the butter and sugar. Stir in the
3/4 cup packed brown sugar      egg, milk and orange zest. Add dry ingredients, mix until well
1 egg                           blended. Then stir in the quick oats, cranberries and nuts.
1/4 cup milk
                                Drop dough by tablespoons onto the prepared cookie sheets about
1 teaspoon orange zest
                                2 inches apart. Bake for 10 to 12 minutes in the preheated oven,
1 1/2 cups quick cooking oats   cookies should be lightly browned. Remove to cool on wire racks.
3/4 cup chopped cranberries
1/4 cup chopped walnuts         In a small bowl, stir together the confectioners' sugar, orange zest
1 cup confectioners' sugar      and vanilla. Stir in the orange juice 1 tablespoon at a time until
1/2 teaspoon orange zest        desired consistency is reached. Drizzle onto cooled cookies.
1/4 teaspoon vanilla extract
2 tablespoons orange juice

                              Cranberry Orange Oatmeal Cookies
Ingredients                       Directions
1 cup butter, softened            Preheat oven to 350 degrees F (175 degrees C).
1 cup packed brown sugar
1/2 cup white sugar               In a large bowl, cream together the butter, brown sugar and white
2 eggs                            sugar until smooth. Beat in the eggs one at a time, then stir in the
1 1/2 teaspoons vanilla extract   vanilla, orange zest and orange extract. Combine the flour, baking
1 tablespoon grated orange zest   soda and cinnamon; stir into the butter mixture. Stir in the oats and
1 teaspoon orange extract         cranberries. Drop by rounded tablespoonfuls onto ungreased
                                  cookie sheets.
1 1/2 cups all-purpose flour
1 teaspoon baking soda
                                  Bake for 10 to 12 minutes in the preheated oven, or until golden
1 teaspoon ground cinnamon        brown. Allow cookies to cool on baking sheet for 2 minutes before
3 cups rolled oats                removing to a wire rack to cool completely.
1 cup dried cranberries

1 teaspoon vanilla extract       Dissolve baking soda in boiling water and add to mixture. Stir in

                                 Drop by teaspoonful onto ungreased cookie sheet. Bake at 350

                                 degrees F (175 degrees C) for 10-12 minutes. Don't over cook.

                                                    Raspberry Oatmeal Bars
Ingredients                       Directions
1 (18.25 ounce) package yellow   Preheat the oven to 375 degrees F (190 degrees C). Grease a 9x13
cake mix                         inch pan.
2 1/2 cups quick cooking oats
3/4 cup margarine, melted        In a large bowl, mix together oats, cake mix, and melted margarine
1 cup raspberry jam              so that it makes nice clumps and there is no dry mix left. Press 1/2
1 tablespoon water               of the oats mixture evenly into the bottom the prepared pan. In a
                                 separate bowl, mix jam with water, and spread over the crust.
                                 Sprinkle the remaining oat mixture evenly over the top.

                                 Bake in the preheated oven for 18 to 23 minutes, or until the top is
                                 lightly browned. Cool before cutting into bars.




                                                6

                            Quick, Easy Fat-Burning Recipes - Click Here
                                        Laura's Easy Oatmeal Squares
Ingredients                        Directions
1/2 cup sliced almonds            Preheat an oven to 350 degrees F (175 degrees C).
1/2 cup butter
3/4 cup packed brown sugar        Spread the almonds onto a baking sheet; toast until the nuts are
1 teaspoon vanilla extract        golden brown and fragrant, about 5 minutes. Remove nuts from
2 cups oatmeal                    baking sheet, and reserve.
1 cup chocolate chips
                                  Melt butter in a small saucepan placed over medium heat. Stir in
                                  brown sugar; cook, stirring, until sugar has dissolved and mixture is
                                  bubbly. Remove from heat. Stir in vanilla and oatmeal. Press
                                  mixture into an 8 x 8 inch baking pan. Bake in preheated oven until
                                  lightly browned and set, about 20 minutes.

                                  Remove from oven, and allow to cool slightly. Sprinkle chocolate
                                  chips over surface; let chocolate sit until melted, 2 to 3 minutes.
                                  Spread chocolate evenly over oatmeal squares. Sprinkle warm
                                  chocolate with toasted almonds. Cut into squares when cool.

                                                                                  Hillbilly Pie
Ingredients                          Directions

                                   Lightly oil a large bowl. Place the dough in the bowl and turn to coat
                                   with oil. Cover with a light cloth and let rise in a warm place (80 to
                                   95 degrees F (27 to 35 degrees C)) until doubled in volume, about
                                   45 minutes.

                                   Grease 2 8x4-inch loaf pans. Deflate the dough and turn it out onto
                                   a lightly-floured surface. Use a knife to divide the dough into two
                                   equal pieces - don't tear it. Shape into dough rounds and let rest for
                                   10 minutes. Form the dough into loaves and place into the prepared
                                   pans. Cover with a damp cloth and let rise until doubled in volume,
                                   about 40 minutes.

                                   Preheat an oven to 350 degrees F (175 degrees C).

                                   Bake in the preheated oven until the top is golden brown and the

4 cups whole wheat bread flour   wheat flour, and salt. Rub in the shortening by hand until the
1/2 cup shortening               mixture is the texture of oatmeal. Make a well in the center, and
2 teaspoons salt                 pour in the boiling water. Mix with a fork until all of the water is
1 1/2 cups boiling water         evenly incorporated. Sprinkle with a bit of additional flour, and
all-purpose flour for rolling    knead until the dough does not stick to your fingers. The dough
                                 should be smooth.

                                 Make balls the size of golf balls, about 2 ounces each. Place them
                                 on a tray, and cover with a cloth. Let stand for at least 1 hour, or up
                                 to 8 hours.

                                 Heat a griddle or large frying pan over high heat. On a lightly
                                 floured surface, roll out a tortilla to your preferred thinness. Fry one
                                 at a time. Place on the griddle for 10 seconds, as soon as you see a
                                 bubble on the top, flip the tortilla over. Let it cook for about 30
                                 seconds, then flip and cook the other side for another 30 seconds.
                                 Roll out the next tortilla while you wait for that one to cook. Repeat
                                 until all of the balls have been cooked. Tortillas can be refrigerated
                                 or frozen.

                                   - the edges should cover some of the fruit and berries, but not
